## 1. System overview

TAQINOR OS is a multi-tenant ERP for a Moroccan solar installer. A browser loads a
**React/Vite single-page app** (`frontend/`). All traffic enters through **nginx**
(ports 80/443), which reverse-proxies three upstreams: the SPA static bundle, the
**Django REST API** (`backend/django_core`, served by gunicorn on :8000), and a
**FastAPI AI/OCR service** (`backend/fastapi_ia`, uvicorn on :8001). The SPA calls
the Django API under the prefix `/api/django/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` and the AI service under
`/api/fastapi/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦`. Django persists everything to **PostgreSQL 16 (pgvector)** and
uses **Redis** as cache plus Celery broker; a **Celery worker** (same Django image)
runs async jobs such as quote-PDF generation. Generated PDFs and uploads live in
**MinIO** (S3-compatible object storage, buckets `erp-pdf` and `erp-uploads`).
Authentication is cookie-based JWT (httpOnly refresh cookie); every API request is
scoped to the caller's `company` (the tenant). The FastAPI service shares the same
Postgres for its OCR (Zhipu) and natural-language-SQL-agent (LangChain) features,
both JWT-protected and key-gated.

Request flow, front to back: SPA dispatches a Redux thunk ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ axios `GET/POST
/api/django/<app>/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` with the JWT cookie ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ nginx ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ gunicorn/Django ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ DRF ViewSet
(queryset filtered to `request.user.company`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Postgres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ JSON back. Quote PDFs
are the exception: the ViewSet hands off to the vendored premium engine (sync via
`/proposal`, or async via Celery) which renders with WeasyPrint and stores the file
in MinIO.

---

## 4. Backend, app by app

All multi-tenant models carry a `company` FK ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `authentication.Company`. ViewSets
filter `get_queryset()` by `request.user.company` and force-assign `company` in
`perform_create` (never read from the request body). The literal tenant field is
**`company`** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â there is no field named `tenant_id`.

> **Contrainte transversale de conception (VX125).** Budget de densitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© de
> signaux : max 3 signaux ambiants simultanÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s par ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cran de liste, jamais deux
> redisant le mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªme chiffre ; badge de maturitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© `<BetaBadge>` sur les modules
> jeunes avec critÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨re de retrait objectif. Voir `docs/design-density-budget.md`.

API prefixes (from `erp_agentique/urls.py`, all under `/api/django/`):
`authentication` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ root, `stock/`, `crm/`, `ventes/`, `parametres/`, `roles/`,
`reporting/`, `contact/`, `installations/`, `sav/`, `records/`, `imports/`
(dataimport), `custom-fields/`, `documents/`, `public/` (tokenized PDFs, no login),
`pos/`, `scm/`, `mrp/`, `transport/`, `douane/`, `ecommerce-connect/`,
`promotions/` (this batch's 7 new apps + the pre-existing `pos`, all `_APP_URLS`).
JWT lives at `token/`, `token/refresh/`, `token/verify/`.

### authentication ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â tenant root, users, JWT  *(path: `backend/django_core/authentication`, NOT under apps/)*
Owns the tenant (`Company`), the user model, registration, and JWT issuance.
- **Company** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `nom`, `slug` (unique), `actif` (bool), `date_creation`; **NTDMO27** adds `tours_actifs` (bool, default True) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â company-wide off-switch for auto-appearing `<ProductTour>` visits (any company, not demo-only, unlike `mode_presentation_actif`). The tenant every other model points at.
- **CustomUser** (extends AbstractUser) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Company; `role` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢roles.Role (nullable); `role_legacy` (deprecated CharField admin/responsable/normal, now kept in sync with `role`'s tier on create/update + a one-off additive data backfill); derived `menu_tier` property = the **authoritative** menu tier read from the *new* Role (AdministrateurÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢admin, ResponsableÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢responsable, Utilisateur/customÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢normal; superuserÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢admin; legacy fallback only when role-less), exposed on `/auth/me/` and the JWT and used by the sidebar; `tier_for_role` + the pure `authentication/role_tiers.py` are the single source of truth; `poste`, `phone_number`, `avatar_key` (MinIO); `is_protected` (owner-account guard), `is_active`, `is_superuser`; **`supervisor`** self-FK (nullable, Feature E) driving team/subtree record-visibility. Record-visibility scoping lives in `authentication/scoping.py` (`record_scope_for`, `visible_user_ids`, `scope_queryset`) and is applied opt-in on the list+detail querysets of crm/ventes/installations/sav (only the new scoped roles narrow; admins/legacy/custom roles see all; users always keep their own records). Buy prices gated by `can_view_buy_prices` (`prix_achat_voir`).
- Endpoints (mounted at `/api/django/`): `POST /auth/register-company/` (public onboarding: new company + admin) Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· `POST /register/` (admin adds user to own company) Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· `GET /auth/me/` Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· `POST /auth/logout/` Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· `POST /auth/token/refresh/` Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /users/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` + `POST /users/{id}/avatar/` (Administrateur + Responsable tier ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `IsAdminOrResponsableTier`, limited tier blocked) Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â· `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /companies/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` (superuser). **PACT116** gives `register-company` its first frontend door ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â public `/register` route (no login, no ERP layout), previously reachable only by direct API call.
- **Tenant lifecycle (SCA18-22).** `Company.statut` (actif/suspendu/fermeture, mirrors the historical `actif` bool both ways) gates login/JWT-refresh/API access ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â enforced in the authenticator (`cookie_auth.py`) and JWT issuance, never a middleware (SCA18, applied where the request already resolves the user ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â zero extra queries). `selectors.active_companies` (SCA19) is the single source of "sweepable" companies for beat fan-outs. `services.close_company`/soft-close (SCA21) moves a tenant to `fermeture` + timestamps `date_fermeture` (30-day grace before purge). A staff-only founder console (SCA22, `IsSuperuserConsole`) at `auth/console/tenants/` (list + usage counts via other apps' selectors, never their models) + `.../statut/` (change actif/suspendu/fermeture) + `.../note/` (free-text `plan_flag`, never billing). **SCA46** adds `Company.benchmarking_opt_in` (opt-in consent, default False) gating the k-anonymized cross-tenant aggregates (`core.benchmarking`). **SCA23** adds a "day-2" tenant test (`tests/test_day2_tenant.py`) as a CI sellability gate: registers a second company and exercises its full journey (quoteÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢PDF proposalÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢accept) in isolation from the first, catching cross-tenant regressions the single-company test suite would miss. **N100/N101 (2026-08-05, the ERP is now FOR SALE):** console gains `auth/console/tenants/creer/` (`views_console_create.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Company + CompanyProfile + first admin invitation + `init_roles` + SCA20 signup hooks, idempotent on slug/email) and the tenant payload gains plan/seats/health/licence-billing (import-guarded reads); `CustomUser.is_taqinor_support` (migration 0027) = editor-support flag granting only the right to REQUEST an impersonation (adminops NTADM22); public `POST /api/django/auth/signup-demande/` (design partners) is parked behind `TENANT_SIGNUP_ENABLED` (default OFF ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 404, throttled + honeypot) and only ever creates a `DemandeInscription` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â accounts are created solely by founder approval in the console.

### crm ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â sales funnel + clients
Leads from creation through funnel stages, client records, Odoo-style chatter,
duplicate detection/merge, reversible archive.
- **Client** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `type_client` (PARTICULIER/ENTREPRISE); `nom/prenom`, `email` (optional), `telephone`, `adresse`; Moroccan IDs `cin/ice/if_fiscal/rc`; `custom_data` JSON. Unique `(company, email)` when email set. **NTCRM14** adds `derniere_alerte_dormance` (timestamp, anti-spam guard on the dormant-account nudge).
- **Lead** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `client` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Client (nullable); `owner` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢CustomUser; `stage` (**STAGES.py keys**: NEW/CONTACTED/QUOTE_SENT/FOLLOW_UP/SIGNED/COLD, default NEW); `perdu` (bool lost-flag) + `motif_perte`; `canal` (META_ADS/WHATSAPP_CTWA/SITE_WEB/REFERENCE/TELEPHONE/WALK_IN/AUTRE); `priorite`, `tags`, `relance_date`; `type_installation` (RESIDENTIEL/COMMERCIAL/INDUSTRIEL/AGRICOLE); energy profile (`facture_hiver/ete`, `ete_differente` bool, `regularisation_8221` bool); **L4** (21/08/2026, + founder extension) phone-script fields: `occupation_jour` (PRESENT/ABSENT/PARTIEL, nullable ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â primes over the founder default in `courbes_journalieres._occupation` when set) and equipment `equip_piscine(_pompe_kw,_heures_jour)`/`equip_voiture_electrique`/`equip_ve_km_semaine(_chargeur_kw,_creneau)`/`equip_clim(_pieces,_kw)`/`equip_chauffe_eau_electrique(_kw,_creneau)` (migration crm/0079 adds the 6 power/crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©neau fields, crm/0080 adds `equip_clim_creneau`/`equip_piscine_creneau` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â every one moves the hourly curve; the tunnel Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Affiner mon profil Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» captures the kW/crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©neau details optionally per checked equipment and the webhook maps them with strict validation, never overwriting an existing value with emptiness; GPS rule (fondateur 24/08): `gps_lat`/`gps_lng` are NEVER overwritten by address-only updates ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â guards in the webhook anti-replay loop and `LeadSerializer.validate`, and the tunnel no longer requires the city when a valid pin is set; all nullable tri-state ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `None` = not asked yet, distinct from `futures_charges`'s paramless web-questionnaire checklist; migration crm/0078; composed into `courbes_journalieres.equipements` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see below); roof/site + pump fields; `source` (OS_NATIVE/ODOO_IMPORT_TEST/SITE_WEB); `is_archived` (bool) + `archived_by/at`; `custom_data` JSON; `web_questionnaire`/`web_estimate` JSON (quote-journey v2: pro/agricole answers without a column + the exact numbers shown to the visitor, whitelisted server-side; migration crm/0062 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the webhook also maps 'professionnel' ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ INDUSTRIEL and writes one automatic chatter note per created lead).
- **Raccordement « aucun »** (QJR-OFFGRID 01/09/2026, migration crm/0085) — 4e choix `Lead.Raccordement` « Non raccordé (site isolé) », partagé par `SiteProfile.raccordement` : inerte pour le filtre de phase mono/tri (`ventes.compatibilites.normaliser_phase` → None), reconnu comme site isolé par `ventes.compatibilites.est_site_isole` → composition hors réseau (voir §ventes QJR-OFFGRID).
- **LeadActivity** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `lead` FK; `kind` (CREATION/MODIFICATION/NOTE); field-change log (`field/old_value/new_value`) or manual `body`; `user` FK; `bulk` bool.
- **QuestionnaireLien** (L-QUEST 25/08, migration crm/0081, TenantModel) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â questionnaire Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  remplir chez soi Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» par lead : deux jetons (`token` client / `token_interne` aperÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â§u muet, POST refusÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), `expires_at` 30 j rouvrable (magic-link), `questions` JSON 3-ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tats, `sections_repondues`. **Whitelist ET ordre d'affichage `SECTIONS_CLES` (recalÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© 25/08 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â engagement croissant, donnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es personnelles en dernier)** : occupation/equipements/energie/toiture/gps/photo_facture/photo_compteur/photo_tableau/**contact**. RÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨gles pures dans `apps/crm/questionnaire.py` (manquantes()/prefill()/appliquer_section() ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ponses mappÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es par le MÃƒÆ’Ã…Â ME extracteur que le webhook site, jamais une seconde validation ; le vide n'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©crase jamais, GPS jamais ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©crasÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) + **grain CHAMP `champs_a_poser()`/`sections_a_servir()` (ordre fondateur 25/08 Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« on ne redemande JAMAIS une donnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©jÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  connue Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â») : `_COUVERT_PAR` retire `adresse` des questions dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨s que gps_lat+gps_lng sont posÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s (le repÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨re localise le toit au mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tre), une colonne connue revient prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©-remplie donc confirmable, et une section sans rien ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  montrer n'ouvre pas d'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cran mort**. Mint privÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© `POST leads/{id}/questionnaire-lien/` (dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut = sections MANQUANTES, chatter Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/mis ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  jour Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â», jamais Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« envoyÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â») ; public `GET/POST /api/django/crm/public/questionnaire/<token>/` (throttle, 404 gÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rique, enregistrement PAR section ; le GET sert `champs` en plus de `prefill`). Page publique `apps/web /questionnaire/[token]` (mobile-first, **6 ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©crans max au lieu de 9 sections ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â regroupement `ECRANS` dans `lib/questionnaire.ts` : les 3 photos sur UN ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cran, toiture+gps sur un autre ; la barre de progression compte les ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°CRANS**, reprise oÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¹ on s'est arrÃƒÆ’Ã‚ÂªtÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, GPS gÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©oloc, photos via intake, POST navigateur via proxy same-origin `/api/questionnaire-repondre` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â un POST PAR SECTION, contrat inchangÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) ; dialogue ERP `QuestionnaireDialog` (workspace lead, patron L-SECT).
- **VisiteExterne** (L-TRACE 25/08, migrations crm/0082-0083 + notifications/0048, TenantModel) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â traÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â§age anti-fraude de TOUS les points publics (visite_site/tunnel_lead/proposition/questionnaire/booking) : `appareil_id` PRIMAIRE (uuid localStorage ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â la MAC n'est pas collectable par le web ; IP = signal secondaire, partagÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e au Maroc), ip/user_agent/langue/duree_s, heartbeat regroupÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, `Lead.appareil_id` (crm/0082). Service `apps/crm/visites.py` (best-effort, jamais un point public cassÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) ; beacon public `POST public/visite/` AUTH = le MÃƒÆ’Ã…Â ME secret que le webhook lead (X-Webhook-Secret, importÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© littÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ralement de webhooks.py) via proxy worker `/api/visite` ; balise site `apps/web lib/visite.ts` (consentement WB29, jamais en aperÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â§u interne). Notifications enrichies (historique de visites ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  la crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ation de lead, IP/appareil ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  l'ouverture de proposition), direction (rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´les Directeur/Administrateur) TOUJOURS destinataire, alertes ROUGES (sÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©vÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ritÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© `critique`) : re-demande de devis d'un appareil dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©jÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  rattachÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  un autre lead + corrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lation concurrent (mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªme appareil sur ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥2 leads sous 30 j).
- **RelanceEtape** (RELANCE FOUNDATION, migration crm/0084, TenantModel) -- plan de relance structure multi-touches par lead (distinct du rappel UNIQUE `Lead.relance_date`/`sync_relance_activity`) : `lead` FK, `ordre`/`due_date`/`canal` (appel/whatsapp/email/visite)/`statut` (a_faire/fait/sautee)/`note`. Gabarit founder-editable `parametres.CadenceRelanceEtape` (cadence neutre par defaut J+2/J+5/J+10/J+20/J+35, seedee au signup + a la volee, API `/api/django/parametres/cadence-relance/`). Services `crm.services.initialiser_plan_relance` (idempotent, pose `Lead.relance_date` sur la 1ere echeance) / `marquer_etape_relance` (fait/sautee, journalise le chatter, fait AVANCER `Lead.relance_date` vers la prochaine etape -- jamais un second systeme de rappel concurrent). API `crm.RelanceEtapeViewSet` (`GET /crm/relance-etapes/?scope=overdue|today|all&owner=`, actions `fait`/`sauter`) + `POST leads/{id}/relance/initialiser/`. Panneau ERP-interne Ã‚Â« Relances du jour Ã‚Â» (`pages/crm/RelancesDuJourWidget.jsx`, monte dans `CrmCockpit.jsx`) -- AUCUN envoi automatique WhatsApp/e-mail, rappels visuels seulement.
- **LeadTag / Canal / MotifPerte** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-company managed lists for tags, channels, loss reasons (each has `archived` bool; Canal has `protege`).
- **WebsiteLeadPayload** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â raw webhook capture from taqinor.ma; `payload` JSON, `processed` bool, `lead` FK (never loses inbound data).
- **Parrainage** (referral program, N98) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `parrain` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Client (the referrer); `filleul_lead` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Lead and/or `filleul_client` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Client (the referred) + free-text `filleul_nom`; `statut` (en_attente/converti/recompense_versee); `recompense` (Decimal, pre-filled from `parametres.CompanyProfile.referral_reward`); `notes`; `created_by`. Feature on/off via `CompanyProfile.referral_enabled`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/crm/`): `clients/` and `leads/` ViewSets (CRUD) plus `leads/{id}/archiver|restaurer|whatsapp-devis|devis-auto|noter|merge|bulk`, `leads/{id}/duplicates`, `leads/doublons`, `leads/historique`, `leads/export-xlsx`, `clients/export-xlsx`; managed-list ViewSets `tags/`, `canaux/`, `motifs-perte/`; `parrainages/` (referrals); `assignable-users/`; `POST webhooks/website-leads/` (public, static secret).
- **management/import_odoo_leads** (N107) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `manage.py import_odoo_leads <path> --company <slug|id> [--dry-run]`: idempotent Odoo `crm.lead` importer reusing the `dataimport` parser (CSV/XLSX) + JSON; forces company server-side, reconciles on normalized email/phone + the existing `(company, external_system, external_id)` unique key (never duplicates), stage names from STAGES.py (unknown ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ NEW). No-op without a file. The real 619-lead extraction stays manual/gated on the actual Odoo backup (PII, never committed). **FG242** adds **ConcurrentPerte** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â on a LOST lead, captures the winning competitor + price/devise/motif (reuses the existing `Lead.perdu` flag, no hardcoded STAGES.py stage); acting user + company server-side, optional LeadActivity chatter note; ViewSet `concurrents-perte/` (`?lead=`). Migration crm/0029. **FG204** adds **PointContact** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a per-lead multi-touch attribution journal (canal reusing `Lead.Canal`, source, date, ordre, optional paid-channel cost) with a timeline + first/last-touch summary selector; endpoints `points-contact/` (+ `attribution/`) and a `leads/{id}/points-contact/` read action. Migration crm/0030.
- **odoo_sync (ODOO-SYNC, 2026-09-01)** -- no-AI bidirectional lead sync with the founder's Odoo (`taqinor-solutions.odoo.com`): `apps/crm/odoo_sync.py` = JSON-2 client (`POST /json/2/<model>/<method>`, bearer API key; env `ODOO_SYNC_URL`/`ODOO_SYNC_API_KEY`/`ODOO_SYNC_DB`, strict no-op without them -- rule #1: never SQL) + sanitize rules proven on the 2026-09-01 production run (placeholder emails purged before matching, Meta-form "street" answers -> note, >20-digit phones -> note since `phone_normalise` is varchar(20)). Commands: `manage.py sync_odoo_leads --company <slug|id> [--dry-run]` (fetch ALL crm.lead incl. archived -> sanitize -> reuse idempotent `import_odoo_leads` -> mirror-align ERP stages on the Odoo pipeline, chatter-traced) and `manage.py push_odoo_stages --company <slug|id> [--apply]` (reverse: writes ONLY `crm.lead.stage_id` via JSON-2, and only when the Odoo stage is inconsistent at the 6-canonical-stage level; dry-run by default). The 18 real Odoo stage names -> STAGES.py keys live in `_ODOO_STAGE_TO_KEY` (import_odoo_leads), founder-validated mapping 2026-09-01. Tests `tests_odoo_sync.py` (fake JSON-2 server, no network).
- **ARC4** sweeps all 13 crm ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour identical, isolation now auto-covered by the generic tenant sweep test). **ARC18/56** add nullable `tiers` FKs mirroring `Client` (migration crm/0050) and `Lead` (migration crm/0051) into the `tiers.Tiers` directory (`tiers_bridge.py`). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/crm/platform.py` (the first platform manifest, alongside `contrats`) declaring `searchable_models`/`record_targets`/`agent_actions_module` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `agent_actions.py` exposes read-only lead/client agent actions.
- **SiteProfile** (DC12, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a REUSABLE per-client energy/roof/pumping profile (OneToOneÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`Client`; taxonomies reuse `Lead`'s choices rather than redeclaring a divergent second list), saved once instead of re-entered on every devis. Endpoint `site-profiles/`; consumed read-only by `apps.ventes` via `selectors.site_profile_for_client` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see the ventes `prefill-site` action (**WIR99**), the action that finally gave this model a caller.
- **LB39** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the VX95 Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Annuler Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» toast on a lead-stage change was PATCHing back to the previous stage and always hitting the funnel guard's 400 (a lead never regresses an ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tape) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the undo was dead in production. `LeadSerializer` now accepts a write-only, never-persisted `undo` boolean: `validate()` allows the regression ONLY when a server-side check (`_undo_of_last_stage_change`) confirms the PATCH is the EXACT inverse of this lead's most recent `stage` chatter entry, within a 300 s window ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â any other backward move stays refused. No model/migration change.
- **NTMIG26** adds a certification layer on **Partenaire** (apporteur d'affaires / sous-revendeur, historical table `compta_partenaire`): `niveau_certification` (aucun/enregistre/certifie/or/platine), `date_certification`, `date_expiration_certification`, `specialites` (JSON), `nb_deploiements_reussis` (denormalized mirror). `services.poser_compteur_deploiements` is the ONE write path for the counter ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â called from `apps.migration` (which owns the deployments table), never a direct `Partenaire.objects.update()` from another app. Migration crm/0069.
- **Dormance & engagement (NTCRM14/15/16/29)** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `management/detecter_comptes_dormants` + `selectors.comptes_dormants(company, seuil_jours=90)` flags signed `Client`s with no recent activity; `clients/dormants/` (list) + `clients/{id}/relancer-dormance/` (nudge, stamps `derniere_alerte_dormance`). `engagement.py` (NTCRM16) is a pure/stateless multi-signal engagement score (0-100: frÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©quence contact/activitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cente/paiements ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  temps/devis acceptÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s, Chaud/TiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨de/Froid labels like `scoring.py`'s lead-scoring) at `clients/{id}/engagement/` + `clients/engagement-bulk/`. `selectors.portefeuille_commercial` (NTCRM29) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a commercial's owned accounts + risk flags, at `clients/mon-portefeuille/`. Frontend: `DormantAccountsWidget.jsx` + `dashboard/PortfolioWidget.jsx` on `CrmCockpit`.
- **SalleVente / SalleVenteItem / SalleVenteVue** (NTCRM17-19) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a tokenized public page bundling several devis/documents/links for ONE lead OR ONE client (never both/neither, `clean()`-enforced); security modelled on `ged.PartageGed` (unpredictable `token`, `expires_at` default 30 d, optional hashed `password_hash`, `actif` kill-switch). `SalleVenteItem` (devis/document/video_lien/note, `ordre`). `SalleVenteVue` logs each public visit (hashed IP only). `selectors.salle_vente_analytics`/`salle_vente_summary_for_lead`; **NTCRM27** `detecter_signal_interet_salle_vente` flags repeat views on a salle linked to an open lead and emits a `salle_vente_signal_interet` domain event (best-effort). Endpoints: `salles-vente/` CRUD (+ `items/`, `DELETE items/{item_id}/`, `analytics/`), `leads/{id}/salle-vente-analytics/`; public `GET /api/django/crm/salle-vente/<token>/` (frontend `/salle-vente/:token`, no login/layout). Frontend: `pages/crm/salle-vente/PublicSalleVentePage.jsx` (public) + `pages/crm/leads/SalleVenteAnalyticsBadge.jsx` (internal, on the lead workspace).
- **Apporteur / DealEnregistre** (NTCRM20-22) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a B2B deal-registration registry, deliberately SEPARATE from the pre-existing `Partenaire`/FG234 lead-submission portal (a concurrent-registration protection window has no equivalent there). `Apporteur` (nom/type/commission %/RIB/`token_acces` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â read-only portal secret, no CustomUser account). `DealEnregistre` (`apporteur` FK, `lead` OneToOne, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete/expire/**a_payer** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â NTCRM22 sets this + `montant_commission_du` on the linked devis's acceptance; `clean()` refuses a second apporteur registering the SAME prospect ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â email/phone/client identity match ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â while another's protection window, default 90 d, is active). Endpoints: `apporteurs/`, `deals-enregistres/` (+ `approuver/`, `rejeter/`, `a-payer/`); public read-only `GET /api/django/crm/apporteur-portail/<token>/mes-deals/` (token-only, never a session).
- **Defi** (NTCRM23/24/28) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a temporary, PUBLIC team gamification challenge (date window + leaderboard), distinct from the private/permanent individual `ObjectifCommercial` (FG39); reuses `ObjectifCommercial.Metric` strictly (no second metric taxonomy). `selectors.classement_defi` ranks users by the dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©fi's metric over its window. Endpoints: `defis/` CRUD (+ `classement/` NTCRM24 leaderboard, `export-xlsx/` NTCRM28), frontend `/crm/defis` (DefisPage, visible to the whole team, not just managers).
- **NTCRM26/30 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â playbook + account-review gap closure.** `Playbook.condition` (JSON `core.rules` condition tree evaluated against `{type_installation, canal}`, empty = applies to every lead) + `selectors.playbooks_recommandes(lead, stage)` (similarity-recommended playbooks, consumed by the pre-existing `lead_playbook_view`). **NTCRM30** closes the backend test-coverage gap on `PlanCompte`/`RevueCompte` (structured account-review notes, scaffolded by NTCRM10/11 migration crm/0063, frontend already wired by PACT105 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â previously undocumented here): a review posted to `revues-compte/` appears immediately, most-recent-first, in the account plan's timeline.

### ventes ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â quotes, orders, invoices, credit notes, payments, quote engine
- **QJR2 contre-visite (31/08, 46 tâches)** : règle « accessoires Huawei hors panier » déclarée UNE fois dans le noyau (`utils/options.est_accessoire_huawei`, builder importe) ; dispatch des renderers en registre déclaratif + paquet **agricole multi-pages** (12 modules, remplace l'ancien one-pager — `agronomy.py` intact) ; `apps/ventes/contracts/modes_marche.json` = contrat des 4 modes de marché (6 sites front/back en parité, garde `scripts/check_modes_marche.py` ; `contract_samples/` reste réservé aux échantillons d'ENDPOINT) ; registre d'overrides verrouillé `select_for_update` sur lire-fusionner-écrire + client API (`lireOverrides`/`poserOverrides`/`regenererOverride`) et panneau « Surcharges » du générateur ; tableau de bord ventes routé par `domain.argent.totaux(Vue.AFFICHAGE)` avec `periode` appliquée aux montants (DV2) ; migration ventes/0109 (réparation `prix_par_kwc` deux-options sans remise).
The largest app: full quoteÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢orderÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢invoiceÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢recovery lifecycle plus the vendored
premium quote-PDF engine.
- **Devis** (quote) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `reference` (per company+month); `client` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢crm.Client; `lead` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢crm.Lead (nullable, lead-primary quoting); `statut` (**brouillon/envoye/accepte/refuse/expire**); `mode_installation` (residentiel/industriel/agricole); `option_acceptee` (sans_batterie/avec_batterie); `etude_params` JSON (kWc, production, autoconso, payback, pump CV/HMT/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©bitÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦); `taux_tva`, `remise_globale`; versioning (`version`, `version_parent`, `superseded_by`, `is_active`); discount approval (`remise_approuvee`, `remise_approuvee_par`); `fichier_pdf` (MinIO key). **FG52** adds `devise` (ISO 4217, default MAD) + `taux_change` to **Devis** and **Facture** (and `parametres.CompanyProfile.devise_defaut`): on API create without an explicit devise, the company default is applied (fallback MAD); the premium PDF `fmt()` and UBL export (`dgi_export.py`/`utils/ubl.py`) emit the document currency. No base-currency conversion (currency is document-borne). **SCA47** adds `prix_par_kwc` (Total TTC ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â· kWc, DecimalField) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â derived and FROZEN write-once at first save when a kWc + total exist (null for kWc-less/pompage devis, never recomputed after); internal generator/BI signal ONLY, same regime as `stock.Produit.prix_achat` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never on a PDF or client-facing output. **SCA39** adds `Meta.indexes` `(company, statut)` + `(company, date_creation)` on Devis and Facture (concurrent `AddIndex`, no blocking lock) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the money-path query subset of NTPLT20. **NTCPQ16** adds `variante_de`/`variante_tier` (self-FK + ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©conomique/standard/premium tag ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a tier variant is a full draft Devis, distinct from the size-variant `version_parent`/QJ15 and the sans/avec-batterie couple). **NTCPQ13** adds `devis_origine`/`numero_renouvellement` (self-FK to the renewal chain's root devis; the source devis is never touched ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â distinct from the T10 revision, which only applies to a not-yet-accepted devis). **NTCPQ11** adds `clauses_appliquees` (JSON snapshot of the applicable `cpq.ClauseCGV` list, frozen once at brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢envoyÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, read-only by the quote_engine, never a new renderer). **TAILLES (26/08/2026)** adds `offres_tailles_config` (migration ventes/0104) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-devis CONFIGURATION of the three explorable installation sizes ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°co/RecommandÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/Max (`{cle: {config, ajuste, modifie_le, modifie_par}}`); it stores ONLY inputs (panel count, battery modules, swapped catalogue products) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â every derived number (prix TTC, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©conomie, payback, couverture, kWc) is re-stamped by the engine on read, so a hand-typed price is structurally impossible. NULL = byte-identical historical behaviour (the engine derives all three). Distinct from `variante_de`/`variante_tier` (NTCPQ16, a full draft Devis) and from the sans/avec-batterie couple: a size is EXPLORATION only ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no lines, no status, no devis created. **MAX RÃƒâ€°EL (28/08/2026)** : sans calepinage MESURABLE (tout devis automatique, layout contour-seul), le compte dessinÃƒÂ©/vendu ne borne plus le toit Ã¢â‚¬â€ Max = derniÃƒÂ¨re taille ÃƒÂ©ligible du balayage plafonnÃƒÂ©e par le MUR PHYSIQUE du tracÃƒÂ© client (`dimensionnement.plafond_physique_du_devis`, aire ÃƒÂ· empreinte, jamais publiÃƒÂ© comme contenance ni comme verdict `toit_ok`, plancher = le champ du devis) ; la contenance mesurÃƒÂ©e reprend la main dÃƒÂ¨s que le 3D est ouvert+enregistrÃƒÂ©. Cartes servies filtrables par lien (voir ShareLink `taille_eco`/`taille_max`) ; page client : cartes en tÃƒÂªte, le dÃƒÂ©tail (hÃƒÂ©ros TTC/ÃƒÂ©co, kWc/panneaux) suit la carte cliquÃƒÂ©e par copie du texte rendu (omission honnÃƒÂªte, restauration exacte sur RecommandÃƒÂ©). PDF rÃƒÂ©sidentiel : rÃƒÂ´le divergent entre options = UNE ligne ÃƒÂ  deux valeurs Ã‚Â« sans Ã‚Â· avec Ã‚Â» + comparatif deux colonnes (`quote_engine/residential/options.py::_pair_divergents`) Ã¢â‚¬â€ devis deux-options divergent tient en 3 pages, non-divergent byte-identique. **LOT 29/08/2026** : (a) le DIMENSIONNEMENT rÃƒÂ©sidentiel passe TOUT ENTIER par le moteur horaire Ã¢â‚¬â€ la rÃƒÂ¨gle des 900 DH est SUPPRIMÃƒâ€°E backend (`build_devis_auto` : cible explicite souveraine, sinon moteur, sinon `AutoDevisError` nommant la donnÃƒÂ©e manquante Ã¢â‚¬â€ facture_hiver/ville/catalogue) ET ÃƒÂ©cran (le gÃƒÂ©nÃƒÂ©rateur prÃƒÂ©remplit via le dry-run `devis/composition/`, plus jamais `estimerPanneaux`) ; (b) endpoints tailles `devis/{id}/offres-tailles/` (GET) + `offres-tailles/config` (PATCH) + `offres-tailles/regenerer` + **`offres-tailles/appliquer`** (POST Ã¢â‚¬â€ la config `recommande` RECOMPOSE LE DEVIS via `sync_devis_from_layout` : brouillon seul, lignes nÃƒÂ©gociÃƒÂ©es refusÃƒÂ©es nommÃƒÂ©ment, chatter, config consommÃƒÂ©e ; Ãƒâ€°co/Max restent exploration-carte) ; dialogue Ã‚Â« Modifier les options Ã‚Â» large (`lw-context-devis-edit-tailles`) dans DevisTab ; (c) options CHARGEABLES sur la page publique Ã¢â‚¬â€ `GET public/proposal/{token}/taille/{cle}/?variante=` (module `apps/ventes/taille_detail.py`, contrat `contract_samples/taille_detail.json`, 404 gÃƒÂ©nÃƒÂ©rique, cache 15 min) sert ÃƒÂ©conomies mensuelles/couverture/banque/cashflow de la taille cliquÃƒÂ©e ; carte RÃƒâ€°CAP RecommandÃƒÂ© en tÃƒÂªte de page quand une seule option est envoyÃƒÂ©e.
- **LigneDevis** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `devis` FK, `produit` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢stock.Produit, `designation`, `quantite`, `prix_unitaire`, `remise`, `taux_tva` (nullable ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ falls back to devis rate; 10% panels / 20% other). **NTCPQ18** adds an optional `lot` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`LotDevis` (NULL = hors lot, historical behaviour unchanged). **L-2OPT (25/08/2026)** adds `variante` (`''` commune | `'sans'` | `'avec'`, migration 0102) : les deux options d'un devis rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sidentiel peuvent porter des dimensionnements DIFFÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°RENTS (ex. 22 panneaux sans / 26 avec) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â composition fusionnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e `services.composition_deux_optimiseurs`/`fusionner_kits`, split PDF/aval variante-d'abord (`quote_engine/builder._repartir_options`, `utils/options.filter_lines_for_option`), payload public `dimensionnement_options`+`production_par_option` (contrat `contract_samples/dimensionnement_options.json`); lignes historiques (`''`) inchangÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es au bit prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨s.
- **NTCPQ13/14/16/18/20 devis satellites** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ConfigurationDevisSnapshot` (auto fine-grained pre-send config history on a brouillon devis, distinct from version revision), `LotDevis` (multi-site devis: groups `LigneDevis` for its own sub-total + a consolidated total; coexists with the QJ29 multi-villa `groupe_index` display grouping), `AvenantDevis` (amendment diff on an already-accepted devis ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `lignes_ajoutees`/`lignes_retirees` JSON snapshots, never `prix_achat`; may re-trigger the NTCPQ7 approval matrix via `approbation_requise`). - **BonCommande** (client order) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `devis` OneToOneÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Devis (nullable), `client` FK; `statut` (**en_attente/confirme/livre/annule**); marking `livre` decrements stock.

- **quote_engine/** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â premium PDF engine. `builder.py` maps an OS Devis ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ the generator data dict (only sell-side `prix_unitaire`; `prix_achat` excluded) and routes by market mode to one of three renderers: `residential/` (redesigned 3-page residential proposal), `agricole/` (4-page pompage-solaire proposal ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â cover/at-a-glance, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tude+schÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ma+charts, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©quipement+prix+FDA+garanties, rentabilitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© solaire-vs-butane-vs-diesel+signature; modules `renderer/render/theme/cover/study/yield_page/economics_page/charts/schematic/economics/constants/sample_data`), and the legacy `generate_devis_premium.py` (one-page + industriel + fallback). `installations.py` = shared cover-hero photo library that picks the installation photo whose kWc is **nearest** the quote (agricole falls back to residential/industriel of similar power); photos in `assets/installations/<mode>-<kwc>.jpg`. `pricing.py`. (`catalog.py` + `catalog_data.json` removed by QJR107, 30/08/2026 â€” `load_catalog()` had zero backend callers; the standalone `services/simulator/` keeps its OWN catalog.py.) Buy-price exclusion asserted by `apps/ventes/tests/test_quote_engine_formats.py`; agricole engine by `test_agricole_quote.py`. The old 3 704-line `test_quote_engine.py` was split (19/08/2026) into `_formats` (page counts/totaux/TVA), `_residential`, `_documents`, `_builder` + `_quote_engine_common.py` (shared fixtures); `test_quote_engine.py` remains as a compatibility shim re-exporting those fixtures.
- **solar_design.py** (FG246/247/249) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â electrical-engineering helpers: `string_design` (distributes N panels across the inverter MPPT inputs, checks string Vmp/Voc at cold temperature vs the MPPT/voltage window, reports the DC/AC ratio), `match_inverter` (picks a compatible catalogue inverter, classification keywords aligned with `builder.py`, never a price-less product), `optimize_orientation` (tilt/azimuth sweep via the existing PVGIS client). Pure + fully tested (`tests/test_solar_design.py`); not yet surfaced in an endpoint. **FG255** adds `ev_charger_sizing` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â EV charging-station (borne de recharge VE) sizing: line current (mono 230 V / tri 400 V ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã…Â¡3), dedicated breaker calibre, charge duration/window-fit, recommended standard borne (3.7/7.4/11/22 kW), and PV coupling (solar surplus feeds the borne first, reporting solar-covered kWh + the lifted autoconsommation rate). Pure math, input-freedom preserved, no model/endpoint/PDF change. **FG256** adds `battery_storage_sizing` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â two objectives: MAX autoconsommation (store daytime surplus, capped by the re-dischargeable night load) vs BACKUP for N critical hours (usable kWh/kW from critical load ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ hours); returns usable + nominal kWh (ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â· DoDÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã…Â¡round-trip), recommended pack and the binding objective. Pure math. **FG257** adds `simulate_bankable_yield` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â financial-grade P50/P90: Performance Ratio = ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â (1ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢loss) over temperature/soiling/wiring/inverter/mismatch/availability, P50 = baseÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€PR, P90/P75 via the Gaussian lower-tail quantile (z=1.282/0.674) of an annual-variability ÃƒÂÃ†â€™ (default 6 %); returns PR, loss breakdown, P50/P90/P75, optional specific yield. Pure math. **FG258** adds `hourly_self_consumption` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-hour min(load, production) over an 8760-h (or 24-h) load curve ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ real autoconsommation rate, coverage rate, injected surplus, grid import; typical residential/commercial load + PV profiles as fallbacks, and `load_curve_from_xlsx` (parses a column via the already-present openpyxl). Pure math, divide-by-zero guarded. **FG259** adds `net_metering_savings` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â values the injected hourly surplus (from FG258) under Moroccan net-metering (loi 13-09): compensated only up to same-tranche import ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ `compensation_ratio` at the tranche tariff (pointe/pleine/creuse), honours the `surplus_injecte_compense` toggle, optional annual cap + residual spill tariff. Pure math. **FG260** adds `tariff_escalation_projection` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a 20ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“25-year financial model: year-by-year escalated savings (ONEE escalation ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ module degradation), cumulative + net, simple & discounted payback year, **NPV (VAN)** and **IRR (TRI)** solved by stdlib bisection/Newton (capped iterations, `None` on non-convergence). Pure math. **FG261** adds `optimize_subscribed_power` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â for C&I clients: net grid demand (loadÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢PV) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ post-PV peak ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ recommended subscribed power = ceil(peakÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€margin) (never above current) + the annual capacity-charge saving, optional kWÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢kVA via power factor. Pure math. **FG262** adds `module_degradation_curve` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-year PV production factor (compound or linear, with a year-1 LID drop) confronted against manufacturer warranty floors (e.g. 90% @ yr10, 80% @ yr25), flagging the first breach year + shortfall. Pure math.
- **courbes_journalieres.py** (CJ1, 21/08/2026) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â builds the public `courbes_journalieres` payload block (kept OUT of the money engine): per-season PVGIS production shapes in LOCAL Moroccan time (`apps/parametres/pvgis_profils.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â 7 reference curves for 13 cities, live DRcalc/PVcalc at the lead's GPS with a 30-day system cache + outage circuit-breaker, honest omission when city unknown), real daily consumption levels from the 12 real bills, `options`/`batterie_kwh` mirroring the quote, `occupation` flag (residential default `presence_jour`, founder field decision). Key absent when no equipment data or no consumption block ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â byte-identical to pre-L4 output (pinned by `test_courbes_journalieres.py::EquipementsTests`). **L4 founder extension** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `_occupation` now consults `apps.crm.selectors.occupation_jour_pour_devis` (`Lead.occupation_jour`, phone script) FIRST: set ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ served flag maps 1:1 (present/absent/partiel ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ presence_jour/absence_jour/presence_partielle, source `lead_occupation_jour:<value>`, the first server-side path that can serve `presence_partielle` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â previously visitor-only), unset ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ unchanged historical default chain (pinned by `OccupationLeadTests`).
- **etude_horaire.py + quote_engine/bareme.py + dimensionnement.py + etude_horaire_view.py** (CJ2a, 21/08/2026) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **THE canonical residential savings engine**, replacing the `AUTOCONSO_SANS = 0.60` forfait (founder order: savings must follow the real consumption curves per season, not a flat 60 % of production). `bareme.py` = bidirectional kWhÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â¡Ã¢â‚¬Å¾MAD **calibrated on three REAL founder bills** (SRM Casablanca-Settat 08/05/2026, 20/12/2025, 20/01/2026): reproduces the 30-day bill to the centime (energy 496,03 / fixed 39,94 / TPPAN 56,80 / total 592,77 + 0,25 % cash stamp), proves the selective rule and the progressive day-prorated TPPAN (art. 16 dahir 1-96-77, VAT-inclusive), adds the TWO fixed lines (meter rental 18,28 HT + connection upkeep 15,00 HT), and inverts MADÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢kWh by bisection on the TOTAL bill (exact on all three). Per-vintage tables with **per-tranche provenance**: T5 2026 corrected to 1,381704 (bill-proven; the repo extrapolated 1,405116 at constant HT) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `pricing.ONEE_TRANCHES` now ALIGNED by QJR26 (founder decision D5, 29/08/2026: drift locks recalibrated by derivation, ERP frontend mirrors updated; the apps/web mirrors are queued as WEB_PLAN QJW12), divergence history kept in `DIVERGENCES_PRICING` (incl. the unresolved T6 2026 conflict). `etude_horaire.py` integrates hour by hour, month by month: real PVGIS production shape ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ the client's occupancy silhouette + L4 equipment layers scaled to THAT month's real bill, overlap via the existing `solar_design.hourly_self_consumption`, battery by chronological daily charge/discharge (`BATTERY_ROUNDTRIP`, starts empty = conservative), money by the two-bill model AT THE MONTH. Returns `None` without real anchoring (rule Z2). `dimensionnement.py` = successor to the founder's Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« 900 DH/month Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» rule: sweeps candidate sizes (granularity = one catalogue panel; upper bound = production/consumption parity + 1 panel, since Moroccan surplus is worth nothing), reuses `composition_residentielle` for the cost AND the founder's 80 % inverter rule (never re-implemented), recommends by explicit named criterion (`meilleur_payback`, then coverage). `POST /ventes/etude-horaire/preview/` (`IsAnyRole`, company-scoped server-side, no writes) is the single source the generator screen will call (CJ2b) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â contract `contract_samples/etude_horaire.json`. Consumed by `pricing.calculate_savings_roi(etude_horaire=ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦)` (block absent ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â¡Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ byte-identical to pre-CJ2a; the seller-entered C&I study still overrides), and the 12 monthly savings become twelve REAL computations instead of the hardcoded `_SF` split. `services.build_devis_auto` sizes EVERY residential auto devis with the hourly engine (founder order 29/08/2026 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â "all sizing should go through the new sizing tool"): only an explicit `target_kwc`/`taille_souhaitee_kwc` bypasses it, the 900 DH rule is DELETED (`_residential_panel_count` is now a kWc-to-panels conversion), and an engine that cannot size raises `AutoDevisError` naming the missing datum (`services._refus_dimensionnement`: facture / ville / catalogue) instead of falling back silently. The three occupancy silhouettes moved server-side into `courbes_journalieres.SILHOUETTES_OCCUPATION` (verbatim from `dayProfiles.ts` with their [A]/[S]/[i] provenance, pinned to the TS file by a source-pin test).

No model; quote-PDF path untouched.

### stock ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â catalogue, suppliers, inventory, procurement
